# Defaults Matrix

> [!warning] Generated note
> Generated by `tools/defaults_matrix.py` from the `Dimensions and defaults` table of each component note. Do not edit it: edit the component note and run the script. CI checks that it is current.

2011 rows from 111 component notes. Lengths are in dp, `padding` is top right bottom left, `font` is size/line height/weight/tracking, `elevation` is the level 0 to 5.

## Elevation

| Component | Part | Token | Default | Compact | Dense | Source | Settable from Python |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [[Accessibility Tree]] | tree | - | N/A: the tree has no depth or shadow | N/A: same as the Default column | N/A: same as the Default column | [[Elevation]] | tess-ui: n/a: the tree is data and draws nothing; tess-shell: n/a: the tree is data and draws nothing; tre: n/a: the tree is data and draws nothing |
| [[Accordion]] | header | `elevation.level_0` | 0 | 0 | 0 | [[Elevation]] | tess-ui: yes (style elevation); tess-shell: yes (Elevation percentage); tre: yes (shadows) |
| [[Active Descendant]] | current row | `elevation.level0` | 0 | 0 | 0 | [[Elevation]] (a selected row is flat) | tess-ui: n/a: element absent in tess-ui; tess-shell: n/a: element absent in tess-shell; tre: yes (shadows) |
| [[Anchored Placement]] | placement | - | N/A: the placed element sets its level; a layer's shadow is its own | N/A: same as the Default column | N/A: same as the Default column | [[Elevation]] | tess-ui: n/a: the placed element sets its level; tess-shell: n/a: the placed element sets its level; tre: n/a: the placed element sets its level |
| [[Application Window]] | window | `elevation.level0` | 0 | 0 | 0 | [[Elevation]] (the OS or the transparent variant owns shadows) | tess-ui: yes (root style elevation on a transparent window); tess-shell: n/a: element absent in tess-shell; tre: yes (shadows on the root of a transparent window) |
| [[Applications Module]] | root | `elevation.level0` | 0 | 0 | 0 | [[Elevation]], the Panel owns the elevation | tess-ui: yes (style elevation); tess-shell: yes (Elevation percentage); tre: yes (shadows) |
| [[Applications Module]] | row | `elevation.level0` | 0 | 0 | 0 | [[Elevation]], the module Tokens section: no elevation | tess-ui: yes (style elevation); tess-shell: yes (Elevation percentage); tre: yes (shadows) |
